# Thatch, round two: the same material, made to read from across a yard

You made this folder's thatch material from `BRIEF.md`; read it and your `REPORT.md` again first. It is good: the bundles, ties, stagger and stems are right at arm's length, the layers agree and it tiles. Now it has been put on the game's wall pieces and rendered, and the renders are in `review/` (attached): `round1_house.png` is a small house from about 10 m, `round1_door.png` a doorway from about 3 m.

What the renders show:

1. **From 10 m the wall reads as stacked yellow boards, not thatch.** Every bundle is the same tone, so at that distance the courses become flat bands and the bundles vanish. The vertical rhythm of bundles must survive at 5 to 15 m: more tonal variation from bundle to bundle (some paler cream, some deeper gold, a few greyer and older), and darker valleys between bundles, a little in the base colour and more in the occlusion and the height, so each bundle's rounded crown catches light.
2. **Every course is the same tone,** so eight courses on a wall look stamped. Make the four courses of the tile differ in overall tone, subtly (about plus or minus 8% in lightness, a little warmer or greyer), as if cut from different batches of reed.
3. **The foot of each course looks like a straight cut.** The geometry gives each course a hard edge, and the painted tips barely soften it. Make the fringe longer and more ragged: tips hanging 5 to 8 cm (70 to 110 pixels) below the course line onto the course below, uneven in length, with gaps between tip clumps through which the darker tied top of the course below shows, and the odd longer stem. The fringe still starts at the same rows; only how far it hangs over the next course grows.

Keep everything else exactly: 2048 pixels for 1.5 m, four 512-pixel courses with their tops at rows 0, 512, 1024 and 1536, stems down the picture, whole bundles per course, the stagger, the mean colour near sRGB (190, 166, 112), no slope or step in the height, OpenGL normals from the delivered height, ORM packing, file names, and the scripts and checks that rebuild and test it all. Keep the round-one result as `round1/` (the four maps) so the two can be compared, and add to `checks/` a side-by-side of round one and round two shrunk to the size a 3 m wall has at 10 m (about 200 pixels for 1.5 m) as well as at full size.

Then add a section to `REPORT.md` saying what changed in round two and how you checked it.

Do not use or imitate pictures from any existing game, and do not search for or download reference images.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild a periodic 2048-square, 1.5 m thatch material.
Requires numpy, scipy and Pillow. No network calls; fixed random seed.

Two original generated sources are retained. The layout is registered to the
four geometric courses, quilted on a torus, and decomposed into pigment and
local relief. Native-resolution epidermal fibres are added to both colour and
height. No geometric course ramp or step is included in height.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import json, hashlib, time
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageCms, ImageDraw
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, map_coordinates, maximum_filter1d, median_filter

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
N=2048
METRES=1.5
HEIGHT_MM=32.
SEED=314159
PERIOD=1150
TARGET=np.array([190.,166.,112.],np.float32)
PROFILE=ROOT/'source/sRGB.icc'
if not PROFILE.exists():
    PROFILE.write_bytes(ImageCms.ImageCmsProfile(ImageCms.createProfile('sRGB')).tobytes())
SRGB=PROFILE.read_bytes()
# Original source control points, measured from the retained image.
SOURCE_TOPS=np.array([60,355,655,948,1245],np.float32)
CENTRES=[
 [49,158,266,375,474,576,678,780,880,981,1090],
 [15,95,205,314,423,530,636,742,849,955,1065],
 [50,160,268,374,477,579,680,786,885,990,1096],
 [15,107,213,318,425,532,640,746,851,957,1065],
]
TIE_Y=[
 [91,103,77,100,86,107,76,104,76,98,109],
 [386,377,393,372,393,375,394,374,392,375,398],
 [686,695,671,693,671,695,673,697,676,698,676],
 [976,966,988,969,988,972,994,971,989,974,995],
]


def smooth(a,b,x):
    t=np.clip((x-a)/(b-a),0,1)
    return t*t*(3-2*t)


def save_rgb(path,a,srgb=False):
    Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(a),0,255))).save(ROOT/path,**({'icc_profile':SRGB} if srgb else {}))


def mincut(error):
    """Top-to-bottom minimum-error seam, allowing one-pixel lateral steps."""
    rows,cols=error.shape
    total=error[0].astype(np.float64).copy()
    back=np.zeros((rows,cols),np.int8)
    for y in range(1,rows):
        prev=np.stack([np.r_[np.inf,total[:-1]],total,np.r_[total[1:],np.inf]])
        choice=np.argmin(prev,axis=0)
        back[y]=choice-1
        total=error[y]+prev[choice,np.arange(cols)]
    path=np.empty(rows,np.int32);path[-1]=np.argmin(total)
    for y in range(rows-1,0,-1):path[y-1]=path[y]+back[y,path[y]]
    return path


def remove_signature_strays(src):
    # Only the unusually long cross-bundle arcs are replaced. Short stray
    # reeds remain. A vertical median reconstructs the underlying epidermis.
    paths=[[(1168,130),(1110,154),(1053,181),(983,216),(925,239),(882,248)],
           [(935,230),(960,259),(982,276),(1011,287)],
           [(870,542),(893,572),(923,591),(944,596)],
           [(420,489),(452,479),(478,469),(507,447)],
           [(185,871),(222,848),(261,822),(302,797),(344,768),(376,745)],
           [(176,1164),(202,1157),(222,1157),(245,1165),(282,1184)],
           [(969,1166),(1010,1167),(1052,1159),(1100,1147),(1141,1132),(1177,1110),(1216,1080)]]
    mask=Image.new('L',(src.shape[1],src.shape[0]),0)
    d=ImageDraw.Draw(mask)
    for points in paths:d.line(points,fill=255,width=13,joint='curve')
    a=gaussian_filter(np.asarray(mask,np.float32)/255,.8)
    replacement=median_filter(src,size=(29,1,1),mode='reflect')
    out=src*(1-a[...,None])+replacement*a[...,None]
    mask.save(ROOT/'source/repeat_cleanup_mask.png')
    save_rgb('source/cleaned_layout.png',out,True)
    return out


def register_source():
    src=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_thatch_layout.png').convert('RGB'),np.float32)
    src=remove_signature_strays(src)
    H,W=src.shape[:2]
    overlap=W-PERIOD
    a=src[:,:overlap];b=src[:,PERIOD:]
    err=np.mean((a-b)**2,axis=2)
    # Prefer a central cut, avoiding tiny slivers at the ends of the overlap.
    err+=((np.arange(overlap)-(overlap-1)/2)/(overlap*.45))**8*10000
    cut=mincut(gaussian_filter(err,(1,.6)))
    mask=smooth(-.65,.65,np.arange(overlap)[None,:]-cut[:,None])
    periodic=src[:,:PERIOD].copy()
    periodic[:,:overlap]=b*(1-mask[...,None])+a*mask[...,None]
    # First register at the final sampling resolution. Course boundaries are
    # exact mesh rows; ragged fringes are permitted to cross those rows.
    yy,xx=np.mgrid[:N,:N].astype(np.float32)
    sx=xx*PERIOD/N
    sy=np.interp(yy[:,0],np.arange(5)*512,SOURCE_TOPS).astype(np.float32)[:,None]
    sy=np.broadcast_to(sy,(N,N))
    tex=np.stack([map_coordinates(periodic[...,ch],[sy,sx],order=3,mode='grid-wrap',prefilter=True) for ch in range(3)],-1)
    # Join the last course body into the original image's overhanging top
    # fringe. This gives continuous stalks at the toroidal boundary, without
    # duplicating a tie or inventing a fifth course.
    y0=1946
    vy=SOURCE_TOPS[0]+(np.arange(y0,N)-N)*(SOURCE_TOPS[1]-SOURCE_TOPS[0])/512
    extension=np.stack([map_coordinates(periodic[...,ch],[np.broadcast_to(vy[:,None],(N-y0,N)),sx[y0:]],order=3,mode='grid-wrap',prefilter=True) for ch in range(3)],-1)
    ov=62
    err=np.mean((tex[y0:y0+ov]-extension[:ov])**2,axis=2).T
    err+=((np.arange(ov)-ov*.47)/(ov*.43))**8*10000
    cuty=mincut(gaussian_filter(err,(1,.6)))
    m=smooth(-.65,.65,np.arange(N-y0)[:,None]-cuty[None,:])
    tex[y0:]=tex[y0:]*(1-m[...,None])+extension*m[...,None]
    return np.clip(tex,0,255),{'horizontal_source_period':PERIOD,'horizontal_overlap':overlap,'vertical_join_start':y0,'vertical_join_overlap':ov,'source_top_control_rows':SOURCE_TOPS.tolist()}


def tied_row(old):
    """Keep the whole binding inside the recessed upper 15% of a course."""
    return 56+(np.asarray(old)-56)*.18


def register_bindings(tex):
    original=tex.copy()
    x=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float32)
    y=np.arange(160,dtype=np.float32)[:,None]
    window=np.sin(np.pi*y/160)**2
    for k in range(4):
        cc=np.asarray(CENTRES[k])*N/PERIOD
        old=np.interp(TIE_Y[k],SOURCE_TOPS,np.arange(5)*512)-k*512
        new=tied_row(old)
        amount=(old-new)/np.sin(np.pi*new/160)**2
        offset=np.interp(x,np.r_[cc[-1]-N,cc,cc[0]+N],np.r_[amount[-1],amount,amount[0]])
        sy=k*512+y+window*offset[None,:]
        coords=[sy,np.broadcast_to(x,sy.shape)]
        for ch in range(3):
            tex[k*512:k*512+160,:,ch]=map_coordinates(original[...,ch],coords,order=3,mode='grid-wrap')
    return tex


def overlap_fringes(tex):
    """Extend each preceding course's reeds across half the next binding.

    Texture transport and the antialiased silhouette occur BEFORE any channel
    is derived, so every colour, ridge, fibre and tip remains registered.
    """
    original=tex.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED+244)
    x=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float32)
    fringe=np.zeros((N,N),np.float32)
    proud=np.zeros_like(fringe)
    masks=[]
    for k in range(4):
        cc=np.asarray(CENTRES[k])*N/PERIOD
        ty=tied_row(np.interp(TIE_Y[k],SOURCE_TOPS,np.arange(5)*512)-k*512)
        # Follow each hand-tied band, with fine staggered broken ends.
        line=np.interp(x,np.r_[cc[-1]-N,cc,cc[0]+N],np.r_[ty[-1],ty,ty[0]])
        jitter=gaussian_filter(rng.normal(size=N).astype(np.float32),2.1,mode='wrap')
        jitter=jitter/jitter.std()*9
        slow=gaussian_filter(rng.normal(size=N).astype(np.float32),11,mode='wrap')
        slow=slow/slow.std()*4
        end=np.clip(line+1+jitter+slow,19,91)
        cy=np.arange(-180,112,dtype=np.float32)
        rows=(cy.astype(int)+k*512)%N
        shift=70*smooth(-180,0,cy)
        src_y=(k*512+cy-shift)%N
        coords=[np.broadcast_to(src_y[:,None],(len(cy),N)),np.broadcast_to(x,(len(cy),N))]
        strand=np.stack([map_coordinates(original[...,ch],coords,order=3,mode='grid-wrap') for ch in range(3)],-1)
        a=1-smooth(end[None,:]-2.2,end[None,:]+2.2,cy[:,None])
        luma=strand.mean(2)
        crest=luma-gaussian_filter(luma,(1,3),mode='wrap')
        stem=np.clip((crest+9)/18,0,1)
        a*=1-smooth(-10,24,cy[:,None])*(1-stem)*.24
        tex[rows]=tex[rows]*(1-a[...,None])+strand*a[...,None]
        fringe[rows]=np.where(cy[:,None]>=0,a,fringe[rows])
        proud[rows]=.9*a*smooth(-60,-5,cy[:,None])
        masks.append({'mean_tip_row':float(end.mean()),'min_tip_row':float(end.min()),'max_tip_row':float(end.max())})
    return tex,fringe,proud,masks


def normals(h):
    mm_per_px=METRES*1000/N
    dx=(np.roll(h,-1,1)-np.roll(h,1,1))*HEIGHT_MM/(2*mm_per_px)
    dy=(np.roll(h,-1,0)-np.roll(h,1,0))*HEIGHT_MM/(2*mm_per_px)
    v=np.stack([-dx,dy,np.ones_like(h)],-1)
    return v/np.linalg.norm(v,axis=2,keepdims=True)


def geometry_fields(tex):
    """Bundle crowns and local tie masks. No slope or step per course."""
    rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    yy,xx=np.mgrid[:N,:N].astype(np.float32)
    crowns=[];groups=[];metadata=[]
    tie=np.zeros((N,N),np.float32)
    for k in range(4):
        cc=np.array(CENTRES[k],np.float32)*N/PERIOD
        widths=(np.roll(cc,-1)-np.roll(cc,1))%N/2
        dist=(xx[0][None,:]-cc[:,None]+N/2)%N-N/2
        nearest=np.argmin(np.abs(dist),axis=0)
        dx=dist[nearest,np.arange(N)]
        cw=widths[nearest]
        variation=rng.uniform(8.4,10.4,len(cc))
        profile=np.maximum(0,1-(dx/(cw*.56))**2)**.75*variation[nearest]
        crowns.append(profile)
        groups.append(nearest)
        records=[]
        for b,(cx,w,ty) in enumerate(zip(cc,widths,TIE_Y[k])):
            Y=k*512+float(tied_row(np.interp(ty,SOURCE_TOPS,np.arange(5)*512)-k*512))
            q=((xx-cx+N/2)%N-N/2)/(w*.36)
            # The generated ties sag slightly. A soft feature gate avoids a
            # hard elliptical ridge. Fine rope relief comes from the source.
            line=Y+4*(1-q*q)
            gate=np.exp(-((yy-line)/9.2)**4)*(1-smooth(.82,1.06,np.abs(q)))
            tie=np.maximum(tie,gate)
            records.append({'id':k*11+b+1,'centre_px':float(cx),'width_px':float(w),'width_cm':float(w*150/N),'source_tie_y':ty,'tie_y_px':float(Y-k*512)})
        metadata.append({'course':k,'top_row':k*512,'bundles':records})
    h=np.empty((N,N),np.float32);bundle=np.zeros((N,N),np.uint8)
    for k in range(4):
        y=np.arange(512)[:,None]
        # Smoothly exchange the two local cross sections under the fringe;
        # their means are comparable and there is no geometry-sized step.
        t=smooth(2,112,y)
        profile=crowns[(k-1)%4][None,:]*(1-t)+crowns[k][None,:]*t
        h[k*512:(k+1)*512]=profile
        bundle[k*512:(k+1)*512]=groups[k][None,:]+1+k*11
    return h,tie,bundle,metadata


def intrinsic_colour(tex):
    """Lift source crevices; transport crest reflectance over each reed.

    Source ridge lighting is used for relief, not left in the pigment image.
    Broad colour and fine epidermal pigment are preserved at reduced strength.
    """
    lum=np.sum(tex*np.array([.2126,.7152,.0722],np.float32),axis=2)
    broad=gaussian_filter(lum,(17,12),mode='wrap')
    crest=maximum_filter1d(gaussian_filter(lum,.42,mode='wrap'),7,axis=1,mode='wrap')
    # Plateau-style shadow lift, rather than a bilateral display sharpen.
    lifted=lum+np.maximum(0,crest-lum-5)*.96
    liftmean=gaussian_filter(lifted,(18,11),mode='wrap')
    pigment=(lifted-liftmean)*.9
    # Natural straw chroma is low-frequency intrinsic colour; normalize the
    # overall palette and suppress dark-gap desaturation changes.
    chroma=tex-lum[...,None]
    chroma-=gaussian_filter(chroma,(20,12,0),mode='wrap')
    chroma=np.clip(chroma,-10,10)*.32
    col=TARGET+pigment[...,None]*np.array([.85,.94,1.03])+chroma
    # Longitudinal source colour lives in real straw, not directional light.
    low=gaussian_filter(lum,(22,1.1),mode='wrap')-broad
    col+=np.clip(low,-16,16)[...,None]*.22
    return col.astype(np.float32),lum


def native_fibres():
    rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED+101)
    # Native 2048-resolution, elongated epidermal fibres. All filtering wraps.
    noise=rng.normal(0,1,(N,N)).astype(np.float32)
    f=gaussian_filter(noise,(8.2,.42),mode='wrap')
    f-=gaussian_filter(f,(.4,1.7),mode='wrap')
    f/=f.std()
    f=np.clip(f,-2.6,2.6)
    # Coordinate warp follows the slight naturally varying bundle flow.
    yy,xx=np.mgrid[:N,:N].astype(np.float32)
    warp=1.2*np.sin(yy*2*np.pi/N*9+.35*np.sin(xx*2*np.pi/N*13))
    f=map_coordinates(f,[yy,(xx+warp)%N],order=1,mode='grid-wrap')
    # Compose 44 independent full-resolution epidermis patches from the
    # close reed stock. Every patch is sampled at <= its original scale.
    stock=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_reed_stock.png'),np.float32).mean(2)
    stock-=gaussian_filter(stock,(4.0,1.1),mode='reflect')
    stock=np.clip(stock,-18,18)/max(stock.std(),1)
    patches=[]
    for k in range(4):
        canvas=np.zeros((640,N),np.float32);weights=np.zeros_like(canvas)
        cc=np.asarray(CENTRES[k])*N/PERIOD
        for b,cx in enumerate(cc):
            w=((cc[(b+1)%11]-cc[(b-1)%11])%N)/2
            xp=np.arange(int(cx-w*.78),int(cx+w*.78)+1)
            dx=xp-cx
            wt=np.maximum(0,np.cos(np.clip(dx/(w*.78),-1,1)*np.pi/2))**2
            sy=rng.uniform(5,350)+np.arange(640,dtype=np.float32)[:,None]*1.35
            sx=rng.uniform(250,1000)+dx[None,:]*1.3+1.3*np.sin(sy/100+rng.uniform(0,6))
            patch=map_coordinates(stock,[np.broadcast_to(sy,sx.shape),sx],order=1,mode='reflect')
            canvas[:,xp%N]+=patch*wt[None,:]
            weights[:,xp%N]+=wt[None,:]
        patches.append(canvas/np.maximum(weights,.001))
    skin=np.empty((N,N),np.float32)
    blend=smooth(0,115,np.arange(128,dtype=np.float32))[:,None]
    for k in range(4):
        row=patches[k][:512].copy()
        row[:128]=patches[(k-1)%4][512:640]*(1-blend)+row[:128]*blend
        skin[k*512:(k+1)*512]=row
    skin/=skin.std()
    f=.72*skin+.5*f
    f/=f.std()
    return np.clip(f,-3,3)


def build():
    t=time.monotonic()
    tex,registration=register_source()
    tex=register_bindings(tex)
    tex,fringe,proud,fringe_meta=overlap_fringes(tex)
    save_rgb('source/registered_master.png',tex,True)
    crown,tie,bundle,courses=geometry_fields(tex)
    tie_uncovered=tie.copy()
    tie*=1-fringe
    base,lum=intrinsic_colour(tex)
    f=native_fibres()
    # No generation/upscale-only claim: this 2K fibre field is new material
    # detail and contributes coherently to pigment and physical displacement.
    base+=f[...,None]*3.8
    cy=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float32)[:,None]%512
    weather=np.maximum(smooth(435,505,cy),fringe)
    base+=weather[...,None]*np.array([-1.,.7,5.5])
    base+=TARGET-base.mean((0,1),dtype=np.float64)
    save_rgb('thatch_base.png',base,True)
    # Locally normalized ridge signal, no low-frequency course lighting.
    local=lum-gaussian_filter(lum,(6.0,4.0),mode='wrap')
    contrast=np.sqrt(gaussian_filter(local**2,(12,12),mode='wrap')+36)
    ridges=np.tanh(local/contrast*.68)
    h=8.5+crown+1.35*ridges+tie*.85+f*.07+proud
    h=gaussian_filter(h,.42,mode='wrap')
    h16=np.uint16(np.rint(np.clip(h/HEIGHT_MM,0,1)*65535))
    Image.fromarray(h16).save(ROOT/'thatch_height.png')
    hq=h16.astype(np.float32)/65535
    save_rgb('thatch_normal.png',(normals(hq)*.5+.5)*255)
    # Gap and overhang occlusion is isolated in red. The source's broad
    # brightness is never copied wholesale to AO or height.
    valleys=np.clip(-ridges,0,1)
    micro=np.exp(-.32*valleys)
    y=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float32)[:,None]%512
    tied_shadow=1-.47*(1-fringe)*smooth(0,22,y)*(1-smooth(55,128,y))
    cavity=np.maximum(0,gaussian_filter(h,(5,9),mode='wrap')-h)
    ao=np.clip(micro*tied_shadow*np.exp(-cavity*.055),.26,1)
    rough=.86+.045*valleys+.013*f+.025*weather
    rough=np.clip(rough,.78,.95)
    rough=rough*(1-tie)+(.765+.012*f)*tie
    rough=np.clip(rough,.75,.95)
    save_rgb('thatch_orm.png',np.stack([ao,rough,np.zeros_like(ao)],-1)*255)
    Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(tie*255))).save(ROOT/'source/tie_mask.png')
    Image.fromarray(bundle).save(ROOT/'source/bundle_ids.png')
    Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(fringe*255))).save(ROOT/'source/fringe_mask.png')
    Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(tie_uncovered*255))).save(ROOT/'source/tie_mask_before_overlap.png')
    Image.fromarray(np.uint16(np.rint((np.clip(f,-3,3)+3)/6*65535))).save(ROOT/'source/native_fibre_field.png')
    save_rgb('source/pigment_master.png',base,True)
    meta={'seed':SEED,'size':[N,N],'metres_per_tile':METRES,'millimetres_per_pixel':METRES*1000/N,'height_full_range_mm':HEIGHT_MM,'height_occupied_mm':[float(hq.min()*HEIGHT_MM),float(hq.max()*HEIGHT_MM)],'geometry_course_top_rows':[0,512,1024,1536],'courses':courses,'registration':registration,'fringes':fringe_meta,'tie_visible_fraction':float(tie.sum()/tie_uncovered.sum()),'generated_sources':{name:{'dimensions':list(Image.open(ROOT/'source'/name).size),'sha256':hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'source'/name).read_bytes()).hexdigest()} for name in ['generated_reed_stock.png','generated_thatch_layout.png']},'mean_srgb':np.array(Image.open(ROOT/'thatch_base.png')).mean((0,1)).tolist(),'build_seconds':time.monotonic()-t,'height_note':'Local bundle crowns and inferred reed/rope relief, plus native 2K fibres. No geometric course ramp or step.'}
    (ROOT/'source/build_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({k:meta[k] for k in ['mean_srgb','height_occupied_mm','build_seconds']},indent=2))

if __name__=='__main__':build()

# Thatch: the wall and roof material of the first building tier

You are making one tiling material for a survival game in which players build bases out of square pieces (foundations, walls, doorways, ceilings) by the thousand. This material is what the cheapest tier is made of: bundled reed thatch lashed to a frame of poles. It is the first shelter a player builds: light, uneven, quick to put up, and it should look it, and still be a pleasure to look at. It covers the faces of every thatch wall, doorway and door, and the top of every thatch ceiling, which is the roof. Thousands of square metres of it will be on screen at once, from arm's length to across a valley.

Work only in the current folder. Take as long as the job needs.

## What it shows

Reed or long-straw thatch hung in horizontal **courses**, like a thatched roof turned upright. Each course is a row of **bundles** standing side by side: a bundle is a sheaf of dry reed stems about 12 cm across, tied near its top with a twisted grass band and hung from a batten, and the stems run downward and end in a ragged fringe of tips. The fringe of each course hangs over the tied top of the course below, so the ties are only half visible in the shadow under it. Bundles in neighbouring courses are staggered by about half a bundle, like brickwork, so the wall reads as bundled, not as one sheet of straw.

- Stems: dry, sun-bleached reed and straw, from pale cream to golden tan, a few darker grey-brown older stems, a little greying toward the exposed tips. No green, nothing orange or saturated. The mean colour of the base colour should be close to sRGB (190, 166, 112).
- Bundles vary: width from about 9 to 15 cm, some fuller, some leaning a few degrees, the odd stray stem crossing its neighbours. The tips at the bottom of each course are ragged and uneven, not a straight cut.
- Handmade and a little rough, not tidy and not dilapidated: no holes, no rot, no moss.

## The layout, which the geometry depends on

- One tile covers **1.5 m by 1.5 m** of wall at **2048 by 2048 pixels** (about 0.73 mm a pixel).
- Columns of the picture run along the wall. Row 0 is the top. Stems run down the picture.
- There are exactly **four courses per tile**, each **512 pixels (37.5 cm) tall**. A course's top edge is at row 0, 512, 1024 and 1536. The game's geometry steps out at exactly those rows: each course is a face sloping outward from its top edge to its bottom edge, and under each course's bottom edge the surface steps back to the next course's top. So the top 10 to 15% of each course is the tied part, set back in shadow, with the previous course's fringe tips painted hanging over it; the body is stems; the lowest part is the start of its own fringe, which continues past the course boundary onto the next course's top.
- Each course must contain a whole number of bundles across the tile, so it tiles left to right; neighbouring courses are staggered.
- Each of the four courses should look different from the others (different bundle widths and leans), while all four belong together.

## What to deliver, all 2048 by 2048 and pixel-aligned (the same stem in the same place in every layer)

1. `thatch_base.png`: base colour, sRGB, 8-bit RGB. Flat and unlit: no baked light, no cast shadow, no highlights, no ambient occlusion darkening beyond the real colour of the stems. The tied part under the overhang is not painted dark here; its darkness belongs in the occlusion channel.
2. `thatch_height.png`: 16-bit greyscale height, white is high. Local relief only: the stems, the rounding of each bundle, the tie bands, the tips standing proud. **Do not** add the slope of each course or the step between courses: the geometry already has them, and a normal map that also had them would double them. Say in your report how many millimetres the full range stands for (about 25 to 40 mm is right).
3. `thatch_normal.png`: tangent-space normal map, **OpenGL convention** (green points to the top of the picture), computed from the delivered height with wrap-around, at the physical scale above, so it agrees with the height.
4. `thatch_orm.png`: one packed picture, linear, 8-bit RGB: **red** ambient occlusion (the shadow under each overhang, between bundles and between stems), **green** roughness (dry straw is rough, about 0.75 to 0.95, the tie bands a little smoother), **blue** metalness (0 everywhere).

Keep the generated source pictures at full size in `source/`, and the scripts that build every layer (`build_material.py`) and check them (`check_material.py`) in this folder, so the whole thing can be rebuilt.

## What excellent looks like

The example to beat is the wood plank wall in the repository's `planning/artist-test/wood/`, which from this folder is `../../../../../../../planning/artist-test/wood/` (start with `checks/layers.jpg`, then `checks/tilings.jpg`, `checks/relighting.jpg` and `REPORT.md`; the pictures attached show it). It is a complete, aligned, tileable set with its own build and check scripts. Beat it: a wall of this should look built by hand from real bundles, hold up at full size, and not look like wallpaper when tiled three by three.

- The 2048 must hold real detail at full size: individual stems a few pixels wide with their own light and dark, not a blurred enlargement of a smaller picture. The image tool returns about 1254 pixels a side whatever is asked; reach 2048 by composing generated elements (bundles, courses) in a larger master, by adding fine detail by script, or both.
- It tiles in both directions with no visible seam: check a half-offset with wrap-around and a 3 by 3 tiling, and fix what you see.
- No motif that repeats so that the eye finds it in a 3 by 3 tiling.
- The layers agree: overlay or set them side by side and look. Relight the normal and occlusion from two or three directions and check that the bundles, stems and overhangs read the right way round.

## Checks to leave in `checks/`

A contact sheet of the four layers; the base colour tiled 3 by 3; the half-offset of each layer; a relit preview from at least two light directions; a 1:1 crop of the 2048 base colour and normal; and a JSON of your measurements (wrap seam against interior differences, normal against height agreement, base colour mean).

## Report

Finish by writing `REPORT.md`: for each file, how it was made (generated, edited, or computed by script), which checks you ran and what you saw, the metres per tile and the millimetres of the height range, how long the work took, and anything you are not satisfied with.

Do not use or imitate pictures from any existing game, and do not search for or download reference images: work from the description above.
The four delivered maps are **2048 × 2048**, cover **1.5 × 1.5 metres**, and share the same pixel origin. Row zero is the top; stems point downward. There are exactly **four 512-pixel courses**, with geometry origins at **0, 512, 1024 and 1536**. Each course contains **11 complete bundles**, giving 44 differently shaped sheaves in the tile. Authored bundle widths are **11.74–14.28 cm**, within the requested 9–15 cm range. Adjacent courses are staggered by approximately half a bundle.

Binding centres were registered into local rows **50.92–61.23**, inside the upper 15% of each course. The preceding course's fringe extends over them. Approximately **61.4%** of the authored binding masks remains visible; coverage varies along each band. The tips have individually varying silhouettes, and their colour is slightly greyer than the protected stems. There are no holes, rot, moss or green growth.

| File | How it was made | Inspection and result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `thatch_base.png` | **Generated source, edited and computed by script.** Registered the source courses and bindings, quilted the two repeat directions, attenuated seven conspicuous long stray arcs, and transported preceding-course stems over the ties. Removed broad illumination, lifted dark crevices and extended crest reflectance across the reed widths. Added intrinsic pigment and epidermal detail, slight tip greying, and a final global colour correction. RGB, 8 bits/channel, embedded sRGB profile. | Inspected the layer sheet, native crop, half-offset and 3×3 repeat. The tied region is not a painted dark stripe. No AO is multiplied into albedo. Final mean: **(190.0002, 165.9999, 112.0001)** in encoded sRGB. |
| `thatch_height.png` | **Computed by script.** Rounded local bundle cross sections, locally normalized source reed/rope ridges, small raised fringe tips, and the same native epidermal field used in colour. Grayscale, **16 bits**. No course slope or geometric step is added. | Inspected beside albedo and in neutral-clay relighting. Bundle crowns are raised; individual reeds, bindings and tips retain local relief. The stored range is 7.403–20.883 mm within the 32 mm encoding. |
| `thatch_normal.png` | **Computed from the saved, quantized height.** Wrapped central differences at 0.732421875 mm/pixel, followed by normalization and RGB quantization. **OpenGL: +X right, +Y image-up, +Z outward.** RGB, 8 bits/channel, linear data. | Independently reconstructed from the delivered height. Angular error: **0.171° mean, 0.316° at the 99th percentile, 0.385° maximum**. Opposing and raking lights change the reed shading in the expected directions. |
| `thatch_orm.png` | **Computed by script.** Red: cavity and receiving-surface occlusion beneath the actual fringe mask, between bundles and between fine ridges. Green: rough dry stems with smoother grass bindings. Blue: **zero everywhere**. RGB, 8 bits/channel, linear data. | Packed channels inspected beside the height and in relighting. Straw roughness averages **0.875**, visible ties **0.781**; total encoded range is approximately **0.75–0.95**. Metalness has **zero nonzero pixels**. |

The full grayscale range represents **32 millimetres**: `height_mm = sample × 32`. A renderer needing centred displacement can subtract its chosen reference height; this does not change the normals. The map deliberately does not use the full numerical range. Its approximately 13.5 mm occupied relief is the local detail on top of the supplied sloping/stepped mesh. The normal calculation is `normalize(-dh/dx, +dh/drow, 1)`, using millimetres on both axes. Enable sRGB only for the base colour, and repeat wrapping on both axes for all four maps.

Two original pictures were produced with the **built-in imagegen tool**, exclusively from the written description. No reference-image search, download, existing-game image, or fallback API/CLI was used. Both returned **1254 × 1254**, and both remain unchanged at that full size in `source/`: `generated_reed_stock.png` and `generated_thatch_layout.png`. Their exact prompts are `generation_prompt.txt` and `layout_generation_prompt.txt` in the same directory.

The final detail is not merely an enlarged 1254-pixel picture. The layout is reconstructed into a 2048 master. **44 separate epidermis patches** from the close reed stock are sampled at 1.3 source pixels per output pixel horizontally and 1.35 vertically, then blended into the four courses. A further deterministic 2048-resolution longitudinal fibre field adds fine variation. The combined field contributes to both pigment and physical displacement. Its prefilter displacement coefficient is 0.07 mm, with an input amplitude limit of ±0.21 mm. These are fine fibres on the larger generated stalks, not a claim of a native 2K photographic scan.

Other files in `source/` are **computed intermediates**: `cleaned_layout.png` and `repeat_cleanup_mask.png` record repeat-mark suppression; `registered_master.png` preserves the final aligned structure; `pigment_master.png` preserves the final albedo master; `native_fibre_field.png` stores the fine field; and `bundle_ids.png`, `fringe_mask.png`, `tie_mask.png`, and `tie_mask_before_overlap.png` preserve construction fields. `build_manifest.json` records physical scales, control points, bundle dimensions, source hashes and the seed. `sRGB.icc` is frozen so repeated builds also reproduce colour-profile bytes.

`build_material.py` rebuilds every delivered map and computed intermediate from the retained originals. `check_material.py` reads the delivered PNGs independently, validates their formats and numerical relationships, and regenerates the inspection artifacts. `requirements.txt` pins the three library versions used here. Rebuild with:

```sh
python3 -m pip install -r requirements.txt
python3 build_material.py
python3 check_material.py
```

For the deterministic rebuild check, run `python3 check_material.py --rebuild`. This runs the builder twice, compares SHA-256 hashes of all four complete PNG files, and then performs the normal checks. The final two builds were **byte-for-byte identical**; results are in `checks/rebuild.json`. All **15** validation checks passed.

The inspection artifacts in `checks/` are:

- `layers.jpg`: the four maps together.
- `base_3x3.jpg`: full **6144 × 6144** base-colour repeat; `base_3x3_overview.jpg` is the smaller viewing copy.
- `thatch_*_offset.png`: full-resolution, half-tile wrapped offsets of every map; the height offset retains 16-bit precision. `offsets.jpg` collects their previews.
- `relighting.jpg`: upper-left, lower-right and raking-right lighting. Each light also has a full-size `relit_*.jpg`; `relit_clay.jpg` removes the pigment cue.
- `detail_1to1.png`: base colour and normal at exactly **one image pixel per texture pixel**. `detail_data_1to1.png` and `detail_relit_1to1.png` show the same area in the data maps and lit/clay views.
- `alignment.png`: source-feature contours over the corresponding colour and height crop.
- `course_layout.png`: exact geometry-origin rows marked over the base colour.
- `metrics.json`: formats, hashes, colour mean, roughness, normal agreement, course-height trends and seam measurements.

I inspected the final 3×3 repeat, layer and offset sheets, native detail, and the three relighting directions. The first procedural study was too smooth and uniform, so it was replaced with the natural generated layout and stock-derived fine detail. Iteration also removed a height discontinuity, made the ties partly hidden, registered their vertical positions, and fixed a roughness discontinuity at the wrap. In the final offsets I do not see a special join at the centre. The horizontal courses remain readable without a conspicuous knot, stain or repeated colour patch dominating the large repeat.

The seam test compares adjacent pixels across each wrap with adjacent pixels inside the image. Opposite edge pixels should be adjacent samples, not forcibly identical. Values below are normalized channel differences, **0–1**, averaged over the whole seam. The 95th percentile is taken over interior line averages, making it comparable with the wrap average.

Fitted body-height trends are only **0.026–0.046 mm per course**, confirming that an additional course-sized ramp or step was not baked into the height.

Remaining limitations: the fine ridge height is inferred from generated surface appearance, and AO/roughness are authored estimates, not measurements or ray tracing. Removing lighting from a generated source is an approximate intrinsic-colour reconstruction; very small pigment/relief ambiguities remain. Some upper fringe contours are more uniform than naturally packed loose straw. A single tile necessarily repeats: the large stray arcs were reduced and there is no dominant stain or knot, but careful inspection can still identify recurring individual sheaves. The 3×3 and lighting checks are offline diagnostics, not an in-engine evaluation of the actual building meshes, mipmaps or texture compression.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independent on-disk validation and visual diagnostics; no material mutation."""
from pathlib import Path
import json, struct, hashlib, argparse, subprocess, sys
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
OUT=ROOT/'checks'; OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
N=2048
FILES=['thatch_base.png','thatch_height.png','thatch_normal.png','thatch_orm.png']


def png_header(p):
    data=p.read_bytes()
    w,h,depth,kind=struct.unpack('>IIBB',data[16:26])
    im=Image.open(p)
    return {'width':w,'height':h,'bit_depth':depth,'colour_type':kind,'mode':im.mode,'icc_profile':bool(im.info.get('icc_profile')),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()}


def font(n=19):
    for f in ['/System/Library/Fonts/Helvetica.ttc','/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf']:
        try: return ImageFont.truetype(f,n)
        except OSError: pass
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def sheet(items,path,cols=2,size=768,caption=36):
    out=Image.new('RGB',(cols*size,((len(items)+cols-1)//cols)*(size+caption)),(23,25,27))
    d=ImageDraw.Draw(out)
    for i,(name,im) in enumerate(items):
        x=(i%cols)*size;y=(i//cols)*(size+caption)
        d.text((x+12,y+8),name,font=font(18),fill=(235,235,228))
        if im.mode!='RGB': im=im.convert('RGB')
        if im.size!=(size,size):im=im.resize((size,size),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        out.paste(im,(x,y+caption))
    out.save(OUT/path,quality=95,subsampling=0)


def srgb_decode(c): return np.where(c<=.04045,c/12.92,((c+.055)/1.055)**2.4)
def srgb_encode(c): return np.where(c<=.0031308,c*12.92,1.055*np.maximum(c,0)**(1/2.4)-.055)


def render(base,norm,orm,light):
    L=np.array(light,np.float32);L/=np.linalg.norm(L)
    diff=np.maximum(0,np.sum(norm*L,axis=2))
    ao=orm[...,0]/255
    rough=orm[...,1]/255
    # Broad dielectric response, intended as a local relief diagnostic.
    V=np.array([0,0,1],np.float32)
    H=L+V;H/=np.linalg.norm(H)
    ndh=np.maximum(np.sum(norm*H,axis=2),0)
    spec=.018*ndh**(2/np.maximum(rough,.1)**4)*diff
    rgb=srgb_decode(base/255)*(.33*ao[...,None]+.82*diff[...,None]*(.7+.3*ao[...,None]))+spec[...,None]
    return Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(srgb_encode(rgb)*255),0,255)))


def seams(a):
    if a.ndim==2:a=a[...,None]
    a=a.astype(np.float64)
    out={}
    for axis,label in [(1,'left_right'),(0,'top_bottom')]:
        delta=np.abs(np.diff(a,axis=axis))
        border=np.abs(np.take(a,0,axis=axis)-np.take(a,-1,axis=axis))
        # Distribution of entire interior line mean differences is a fair
        # comparison with the mean difference along the wrap line.
        reduce=(0,2) if axis==1 else (1,2)
        lines=delta.mean(axis=reduce)
        out[label]={'wrap_mean':float(border.mean()),'interior_mean':float(lines.mean()),'interior_line_p95':float(np.percentile(lines,95)),'interior_line_p99':float(np.percentile(lines,99)),'ratio_to_interior_mean':float(border.mean()/max(lines.mean(),1e-9)),'wrap_pixel_p95':float(np.percentile(border,95))}
    return out


def main():
    meta=json.loads((ROOT/'source/build_manifest.json').read_text())
    headers={f:png_header(ROOT/f) for f in FILES}
    ims={f:Image.open(ROOT/f) for f in FILES}
    arrays={f:np.array(im) for f,im in ims.items()}
    base=arrays['thatch_base.png'].astype(np.float32)
    raw_h=arrays['thatch_height.png']
    h=raw_h.astype(np.float64)/65535
    normal=arrays['thatch_normal.png'].astype(np.float64)/255*2-1
    lengths=np.linalg.norm(normal,axis=2)
    normal/=lengths[...,None]
    orm=arrays['thatch_orm.png']
    spacing=1500/N
    dx=(np.roll(h,-1,1)-np.roll(h,1,1))*32/(2*spacing)
    dy=(np.roll(h,-1,0)-np.roll(h,1,0))*32/(2*spacing)
    ref=np.stack((-dx,dy,np.ones_like(h)),axis=2)
    ref/=np.linalg.norm(ref,axis=2,keepdims=True)
    angle=np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip(np.sum(ref*normal,axis=2),-1,1)))
    tie=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/tie_mask.png'))>128
    master=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/registered_master.png'))
    fringe=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/fringe_mask.png'))>128
    ids=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/bundle_ids.png'))
    seam={f:seams(a.astype(np.float32)/(65535 if f=='thatch_height.png' else 255)) for f,a in arrays.items()}
    row_means=(h*32).mean(1)
    body_slopes=[]
    for k in range(4):
        y=np.arange(120,470)
        body_slopes.append(float(np.polyfit(y,row_means[k*512+y],1)[0]*512))
    mean=base.mean((0,1),dtype=np.float64)
    tests={
        'dimensions':all(v['width']==N and v['height']==N for v in headers.values()),
        'height_16bit_greyscale':headers['thatch_height.png']['bit_depth']==16 and headers['thatch_height.png']['colour_type']==0,
        'rgb_8bit':all(headers[f]['bit_depth']==8 and headers[f]['colour_type']==2 for f in [FILES[0],FILES[2],FILES[3]]),
        'srgb_on_base_only':headers[FILES[0]]['icc_profile'] and not any(headers[f]['icc_profile'] for f in FILES[1:]),
        'metalness_zero':bool(np.max(orm[...,2])==0),
        'colour_mean_within_one_code':bool(np.max(np.abs(mean-[190,166,112]))<1),
        'normal_quantization_error_below_half_degree':bool(angle.max()<.5),
        'bundle_id_coverage':bool((ids==0).sum()==0),
        'course_origins':meta['geometry_course_top_rows']==[0,512,1024,1536],
        '11_bundles_per_course':all(len(c['bundles'])==11 for c in meta['courses']),
        'bundle_widths_9_to_15_cm':all(9<=b['width_cm']<=15.02 for c in meta['courses'] for b in c['bundles']),
        'body_no_course_ramp':all(abs(v)<.2 for v in body_slopes),
        'seams_within_interior_p95':all(v['wrap_mean']<=v['interior_line_p95'] for m in seam.values() for v in m.values()),
        'ties_partly_hidden':.35<meta['tie_visible_fraction']<.7,
        'bindings_within_upper_15_percent':all(35<=b['tie_y_px']<=65 for c in meta['courses'] for b in c['bundles']),
    }
    metrics={'units':'Seam statistics are normalized 0..1 channel differences. Height in millimetres. Normal errors in degrees.', 'files':headers,'physical':{'metres_per_tile':1.5,'millimetres_per_pixel':spacing,'height_full_range_mm':32,'height_used_mm':[float(h.min()*32),float(h.max()*32)]},'base_colour_mean_srgb_0_255':mean.tolist(),'base_colour_channel_std':base.std((0,1),dtype=np.float64).tolist(),'base_tied_region_mean':base[np.indices((N,N))[0]%512<75].mean(0).tolist(),'base_body_mean':base[(np.indices((N,N))[0]%512>150)&(np.indices((N,N))[0]%512<425)].mean(0).tolist(),'seams':seam,'normal_height_agreement_degrees':{'mean':float(angle.mean()),'p99':float(np.percentile(angle,99)),'max':float(angle.max())},'decoded_normal_length':{'mean':float(lengths.mean()),'min':float(lengths.min()),'max':float(lengths.max())},'metalness_nonzero_pixels':int(np.count_nonzero(orm[...,2])),'roughness':{'range':(np.array([orm[...,1].min(),orm[...,1].max()])/255).tolist(),'mean':float(orm[...,1].mean()/255),'ties_mean':float(orm[...,1][tie].mean()/255),'straw_mean':float(orm[...,1][~tie].mean()/255)},'visible_tie_pixels':int(tie.sum()),'visible_fraction_of_authored_tie_masks':meta['tie_visible_fraction'],'fringe_pixels':int(fringe.sum()),'unique_bundle_ids':len(np.unique(ids)),'unassigned_bundle_id_pixels':int((ids==0).sum()),'body_height_trend_mm_per_course':body_slopes,'mean_height_jump_at_course_origins_mm':[float(row_means[i]-row_means[(i-1)%N]) for i in [0,512,1024,1536]],'tests':tests}
    (OUT/'metrics.json').write_text(json.dumps(metrics,indent=2)+'\n')
    display={f:(Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(h*255))).convert('RGB') if f=='thatch_height.png' else im.convert('RGB')) for f,im in ims.items()}
    sheet([(f.replace('thatch_',''),display[f]) for f in FILES],'layers.jpg')
    layout=display[FILES[0]].copy()
    ld=ImageDraw.Draw(layout)
    for y in [0,512,1024,1536]:
        ld.line((0,y,N-1,y),fill=(25,105,200),width=2)
        ld.rectangle((10,y+9,445,y+48),fill=(24,28,30))
        ld.text((20,y+14),f'Course {y//512+1}: geometry origin row {y}',font=font(23),fill=(245,245,235))
    layout.save(OUT/'course_layout.png')
    offsets=[]
    for f in FILES:
        im=Image.fromarray(np.roll(arrays[f],(N//2,N//2),(0,1)))
        im.save(OUT/f.replace('.png','_offset.png'),**({'icc_profile':ims[f].info['icc_profile']} if f==FILES[0] else {}))
        viz=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(np.array(im)/257))).convert('RGB') if f==FILES[1] else im.convert('RGB')
        offsets.append((f.replace('thatch_','')+' | half offset',viz))
    sheet(offsets,'offsets.jpg')
    tiled=np.tile(arrays[FILES[0]],(3,3,1))
    Image.fromarray(tiled).save(OUT/'base_3x3.jpg',quality=95,subsampling=0)
    Image.fromarray(tiled).resize((2048,2048),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS).save(OUT/'base_3x3_overview.jpg',quality=95,subsampling=0)
    lights=[('upper_left',[-.65,.65,.72]),('lower_right',[.65,-.55,.72]),('raking_right',[.91,.12,.40])]
    relit=[]
    for name,light in lights:
        im=render(base,normal.astype(np.float32),orm,light)
        im.save(OUT/f'relit_{name}.jpg',quality=97,subsampling=0)
        relit.append((name.replace('_',' ')+' | normal + AO',im))
    sheet(relit,'relighting.jpg',3,768)
    # Neutral clay reveals relief without any base-colour cue.
    clay=render(np.full_like(base,183),normal.astype(np.float32),orm,[-.65,.65,.72])
    clay.save(OUT/'relit_clay.jpg',quality=97,subsampling=0)
    # The exact crop is not resized; 1 output pixel == 1 texture pixel.
    box=(600,430,1368,1198)
    sheet([('base colour | native 1:1',display[FILES[0]].crop(box)),('OpenGL normal | native 1:1',display[FILES[2]].crop(box))],'detail_1to1.png',2,768)
    sheet([('height | native 1:1',display[FILES[1]].crop(box)),('ORM | native 1:1',display[FILES[3]].crop(box))],'detail_data_1to1.png',2,768)
    sheet([('lit | native 1:1',relit[0][1].crop(box)),('clay | native 1:1',clay.crop(box))],'detail_relit_1to1.png',2,768)
    # Shared ID edge overlay helps detect feature registration errors.
    lum=master.astype(np.float32).mean(2)
    edge=(np.abs(np.roll(lum,1,1)-lum)>26)&(np.abs(np.roll(lum,1,0)-lum)<10)
    overlay=np.uint8(base.copy()); overlay[edge]=np.uint8(.58*overlay[edge]+.42*np.array([31,125,204]))
    sheet([('source stem edges / albedo',Image.fromarray(overlay).crop(box)),('height with source edges',Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.asarray(display[FILES[1]])*.7+np.asarray(Image.fromarray(overlay))*.3)).crop(box))],'alignment.png',2,768)
    print(json.dumps({'tests':tests,'mean':mean.tolist(),'normal_degrees':metrics['normal_height_agreement_degrees'],'body_trend_mm':body_slopes,'seam_ratios':{f:{d:round(s['ratio_to_interior_mean'],3) for d,s in v.items()} for f,v in seam.items()}},indent=2))
    if not all(tests.values()): raise SystemExit('Validation failed; see checks/metrics.json')

if __name__=='__main__':
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--rebuild',action='store_true',help='Also verify that rebuilding reproduces all four PNGs byte for byte.')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    if args.rebuild:
        # First build also fixes any stale outputs before the comparison.
        hashes=[]
        for _ in range(2):
            subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(ROOT/'build_material.py')],cwd=ROOT,check=True)
            hashes.append({f:hashlib.sha256((ROOT/f).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for f in FILES})
        comparison={'identical_png_files':hashes[0]==hashes[1],'first_build_sha256':hashes[0],'second_build_sha256':hashes[1]}
        (OUT/'rebuild.json').write_text(json.dumps(comparison,indent=2)+'\n')
        if not comparison['identical_png_files']:raise SystemExit('Rebuild is not deterministic')
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild a periodic 2048-square, 1.5 m thatch material.
Requires numpy, scipy and Pillow. No network calls; fixed random seed.

Two original generated sources are retained. The layout is registered to the
four geometric courses, quilted on a torus, and decomposed into pigment and
local relief. Native-resolution epidermal fibres are added to both colour and
height. No geometric course ramp or step is included in height.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import json, hashlib, time
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageCms, ImageDraw
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, map_coordinates, maximum_filter1d, median_filter

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
N=2048
METRES=1.5
HEIGHT_MM=32.
SEED=314159
PERIOD=1150
TARGET=np.array([190.,166.,112.],np.float32)
PROFILE=ROOT/'source/sRGB.icc'
if not PROFILE.exists():
    PROFILE.write_bytes(ImageCms.ImageCmsProfile(ImageCms.createProfile('sRGB')).tobytes())
SRGB=PROFILE.read_bytes()
# Original source control points, measured from the retained image.
SOURCE_TOPS=np.array([60,355,655,948,1245],np.float32)
CENTRES=[
 [49,158,266,375,474,576,678,780,880,981,1090],
 [15,95,205,314,423,530,636,742,849,955,1065],
 [50,160,268,374,477,579,680,786,885,990,1096],
 [15,107,213,318,425,532,640,746,851,957,1065],
]
TIE_Y=[
 [91,103,77,100,86,107,76,104,76,98,109],
 [386,377,393,372,393,375,394,374,392,375,398],
 [686,695,671,693,671,695,673,697,676,698,676],
 [976,966,988,969,988,972,994,971,989,974,995],
]


def smooth(a,b,x):
    t=np.clip((x-a)/(b-a),0,1)
    return t*t*(3-2*t)


def save_rgb(path,a,srgb=False):
    Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(a),0,255))).save(ROOT/path,**({'icc_profile':SRGB} if srgb else {}))


def mincut(error):
    """Top-to-bottom minimum-error seam, allowing one-pixel lateral steps."""
    rows,cols=error.shape
    total=error[0].astype(np.float64).copy()
    back=np.zeros((rows,cols),np.int8)
    for y in range(1,rows):
        prev=np.stack([np.r_[np.inf,total[:-1]],total,np.r_[total[1:],np.inf]])
        choice=np.argmin(prev,axis=0)
        back[y]=choice-1
        total=error[y]+prev[choice,np.arange(cols)]
    path=np.empty(rows,np.int32);path[-1]=np.argmin(total)
    for y in range(rows-1,0,-1):path[y-1]=path[y]+back[y,path[y]]
    return path


def remove_signature_strays(src):
    # Only the unusually long cross-bundle arcs are replaced. Short stray
    # reeds remain. A vertical median reconstructs the underlying epidermis.
    paths=[[(1168,130),(1110,154),(1053,181),(983,216),(925,239),(882,248)],
           [(935,230),(960,259),(982,276),(1011,287)],
           [(870,542),(893,572),(923,591),(944,596)],
           [(420,489),(452,479),(478,469),(507,447)],
           [(185,871),(222,848),(261,822),(302,797),(344,768),(376,745)],
           [(176,1164),(202,1157),(222,1157),(245,1165),(282,1184)],
           [(969,1166),(1010,1167),(1052,1159),(1100,1147),(1141,1132),(1177,1110),(1216,1080)]]
    mask=Image.new('L',(src.shape[1],src.shape[0]),0)
    d=ImageDraw.Draw(mask)
    for points in paths:d.line(points,fill=255,width=13,joint='curve')
    a=gaussian_filter(np.asarray(mask,np.float32)/255,.8)
    replacement=median_filter(src,size=(29,1,1),mode='reflect')
    out=src*(1-a[...,None])+replacement*a[...,None]
    mask.save(ROOT/'source/repeat_cleanup_mask.png')
    save_rgb('source/cleaned_layout.png',out,True)
    return out


def register_source():
    src=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_thatch_layout.png').convert('RGB'),np.float32)
    src=remove_signature_strays(src)
    H,W=src.shape[:2]
    overlap=W-PERIOD
    a=src[:,:overlap];b=src[:,PERIOD:]
    err=np.mean((a-b)**2,axis=2)
    # Prefer a central cut, avoiding tiny slivers at the ends of the overlap.
    err+=((np.arange(overlap)-(overlap-1)/2)/(overlap*.45))**8*10000
    cut=mincut(gaussian_filter(err,(1,.6)))
    mask=smooth(-.65,.65,np.arange(overlap)[None,:]-cut[:,None])
    periodic=src[:,:PERIOD].copy()
    periodic[:,:overlap]=b*(1-mask[...,None])+a*mask[...,None]
    # First register at the final sampling resolution. Course boundaries are
    # exact mesh rows; ragged fringes are permitted to cross those rows.
    yy,xx=np.mgrid[:N,:N].astype(np.float32)
    sx=xx*PERIOD/N
    sy=np.interp(yy[:,0],np.arange(5)*512,SOURCE_TOPS).astype(np.float32)[:,None]
    sy=np.broadcast_to(sy,(N,N))
    tex=np.stack([map_coordinates(periodic[...,ch],[sy,sx],order=3,mode='grid-wrap',prefilter=True) for ch in range(3)],-1)
    # Join the last course body into the original image's overhanging top
    # fringe. This gives continuous stalks at the toroidal boundary, without
    # duplicating a tie or inventing a fifth course.
    y0=1946
    vy=SOURCE_TOPS[0]+(np.arange(y0,N)-N)*(SOURCE_TOPS[1]-SOURCE_TOPS[0])/512
    extension=np.stack([map_coordinates(periodic[...,ch],[np.broadcast_to(vy[:,None],(N-y0,N)),sx[y0:]],order=3,mode='grid-wrap',prefilter=True) for ch in range(3)],-1)
    ov=62
    err=np.mean((tex[y0:y0+ov]-extension[:ov])**2,axis=2).T
    err+=((np.arange(ov)-ov*.47)/(ov*.43))**8*10000
    cuty=mincut(gaussian_filter(err,(1,.6)))
    m=smooth(-.65,.65,np.arange(N-y0)[:,None]-cuty[None,:])
    tex[y0:]=tex[y0:]*(1-m[...,None])+extension*m[...,None]
    return np.clip(tex,0,255),{'horizontal_source_period':PERIOD,'horizontal_overlap':overlap,'vertical_join_start':y0,'vertical_join_overlap':ov,'source_top_control_rows':SOURCE_TOPS.tolist()}


def tied_row(old):
    """Keep the whole binding inside the recessed upper 15% of a course."""
    return 56+(np.asarray(old)-56)*.18


def register_bindings(tex):
    original=tex.copy()
    x=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float32)
    y=np.arange(160,dtype=np.float32)[:,None]
    window=np.sin(np.pi*y/160)**2
    for k in range(4):
        cc=np.asarray(CENTRES[k])*N/PERIOD
        old=np.interp(TIE_Y[k],SOURCE_TOPS,np.arange(5)*512)-k*512
        new=tied_row(old)
        amount=(old-new)/np.sin(np.pi*new/160)**2
        offset=np.interp(x,np.r_[cc[-1]-N,cc,cc[0]+N],np.r_[amount[-1],amount,amount[0]])
        sy=k*512+y+window*offset[None,:]
        coords=[sy,np.broadcast_to(x,sy.shape)]
        for ch in range(3):
            tex[k*512:k*512+160,:,ch]=map_coordinates(original[...,ch],coords,order=3,mode='grid-wrap')
    return tex


def overlap_fringes(tex):
    """Extend each preceding course's reeds across half the next binding.

    Texture transport and the antialiased silhouette occur BEFORE any channel
    is derived, so every colour, ridge, fibre and tip remains registered.
    """
    original=tex.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED+244)
    x=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float32)
    fringe=np.zeros((N,N),np.float32)
    proud=np.zeros_like(fringe)
    masks=[]
    for k in range(4):
        cc=np.asarray(CENTRES[k])*N/PERIOD
        ty=tied_row(np.interp(TIE_Y[k],SOURCE_TOPS,np.arange(5)*512)-k*512)
        # Follow each hand-tied band, with fine staggered broken ends.
        line=np.interp(x,np.r_[cc[-1]-N,cc,cc[0]+N],np.r_[ty[-1],ty,ty[0]])
        jitter=gaussian_filter(rng.normal(size=N).astype(np.float32),2.1,mode='wrap')
        jitter=jitter/jitter.std()*9
        slow=gaussian_filter(rng.normal(size=N).astype(np.float32),11,mode='wrap')
        slow=slow/slow.std()*4
        end=np.clip(line+1+jitter+slow,19,91)
        cy=np.arange(-180,112,dtype=np.float32)
        rows=(cy.astype(int)+k*512)%N
        shift=70*smooth(-180,0,cy)
        src_y=(k*512+cy-shift)%N
        coords=[np.broadcast_to(src_y[:,None],(len(cy),N)),np.broadcast_to(x,(len(cy),N))]
        strand=np.stack([map_coordinates(original[...,ch],coords,order=3,mode='grid-wrap') for ch in range(3)],-1)
        a=1-smooth(end[None,:]-2.2,end[None,:]+2.2,cy[:,None])
        luma=strand.mean(2)
        crest=luma-gaussian_filter(luma,(1,3),mode='wrap')
        stem=np.clip((crest+9)/18,0,1)
        a*=1-smooth(-10,24,cy[:,None])*(1-stem)*.24
        tex[rows]=tex[rows]*(1-a[...,None])+strand*a[...,None]
        fringe[rows]=np.where(cy[:,None]>=0,a,fringe[rows])
        proud[rows]=.9*a*smooth(-60,-5,cy[:,None])
        masks.append({'mean_tip_row':float(end.mean()),'min_tip_row':float(end.min()),'max_tip_row':float(end.max())})
    return tex,fringe,proud,masks


def normals(h):
    mm_per_px=METRES*1000/N
    dx=(np.roll(h,-1,1)-np.roll(h,1,1))*HEIGHT_MM/(2*mm_per_px)
    dy=(np.roll(h,-1,0)-np.roll(h,1,0))*HEIGHT_MM/(2*mm_per_px)
    v=np.stack([-dx,dy,np.ones_like(h)],-1)
    return v/np.linalg.norm(v,axis=2,keepdims=True)


def geometry_fields(tex):
    """Bundle crowns and local tie masks. No slope or step per course."""
    rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    yy,xx=np.mgrid[:N,:N].astype(np.float32)
    crowns=[];groups=[];metadata=[]
    tie=np.zeros((N,N),np.float32)
    for k in range(4):
        cc=np.array(CENTRES[k],np.float32)*N/PERIOD
        widths=(np.roll(cc,-1)-np.roll(cc,1))%N/2
        dist=(xx[0][None,:]-cc[:,None]+N/2)%N-N/2
        nearest=np.argmin(np.abs(dist),axis=0)
        dx=dist[nearest,np.arange(N)]
        cw=widths[nearest]
        variation=rng.uniform(8.4,10.4,len(cc))
        profile=np.maximum(0,1-(dx/(cw*.56))**2)**.75*variation[nearest]
        crowns.append(profile)
        groups.append(nearest)
        records=[]
        for b,(cx,w,ty) in enumerate(zip(cc,widths,TIE_Y[k])):
            Y=k*512+float(tied_row(np.interp(ty,SOURCE_TOPS,np.arange(5)*512)-k*512))
            q=((xx-cx+N/2)%N-N/2)/(w*.36)
            # The generated ties sag slightly. A soft feature gate avoids a
            # hard elliptical ridge. Fine rope relief comes from the source.
            line=Y+4*(1-q*q)
            gate=np.exp(-((yy-line)/9.2)**4)*(1-smooth(.82,1.06,np.abs(q)))
            tie=np.maximum(tie,gate)
            records.append({'id':k*11+b+1,'centre_px':float(cx),'width_px':float(w),'width_cm':float(w*150/N),'source_tie_y':ty,'tie_y_px':float(Y-k*512)})
        metadata.append({'course':k,'top_row':k*512,'bundles':records})
    h=np.empty((N,N),np.float32);bundle=np.zeros((N,N),np.uint8)
    for k in range(4):
        y=np.arange(512)[:,None]
        # Smoothly exchange the two local cross sections under the fringe;
        # their means are comparable and there is no geometry-sized step.
        t=smooth(2,112,y)
        profile=crowns[(k-1)%4][None,:]*(1-t)+crowns[k][None,:]*t
        h[k*512:(k+1)*512]=profile
        bundle[k*512:(k+1)*512]=groups[k][None,:]+1+k*11
    return h,tie,bundle,metadata


def intrinsic_colour(tex):
    """Lift source crevices; transport crest reflectance over each reed.

    Source ridge lighting is used for relief, not left in the pigment image.
    Broad colour and fine epidermal pigment are preserved at reduced strength.
    """
    lum=np.sum(tex*np.array([.2126,.7152,.0722],np.float32),axis=2)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Independent on-disk validation and visual diagnostics; no material mutation."""
from pathlib import Path
import json, struct, hashlib, argparse, subprocess, sys
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
OUT=ROOT/'checks'; OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
N=2048
FILES=['thatch_base.png','thatch_height.png','thatch_normal.png','thatch_orm.png']


def png_header(p):
    data=p.read_bytes()
    w,h,depth,kind=struct.unpack('>IIBB',data[16:26])
    im=Image.open(p)
    return {'width':w,'height':h,'bit_depth':depth,'colour_type':kind,'mode':im.mode,'icc_profile':bool(im.info.get('icc_profile')),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()}


def font(n=19):
    for f in ['/System/Library/Fonts/Helvetica.ttc','/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf']:
        try: return ImageFont.truetype(f,n)
        except OSError: pass
    return ImageFont.load_default()


def sheet(items,path,cols=2,size=768,caption=36):
    out=Image.new('RGB',(cols*size,((len(items)+cols-1)//cols)*(size+caption)),(23,25,27))
    d=ImageDraw.Draw(out)
    for i,(name,im) in enumerate(items):
        x=(i%cols)*size;y=(i//cols)*(size+caption)
        d.text((x+12,y+8),name,font=font(18),fill=(235,235,228))
        if im.mode!='RGB': im=im.convert('RGB')
        if im.size!=(size,size):im=im.resize((size,size),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)
        out.paste(im,(x,y+caption))
    out.save(OUT/path,quality=95,subsampling=0)


def srgb_decode(c): return np.where(c<=.04045,c/12.92,((c+.055)/1.055)**2.4)
def srgb_encode(c): return np.where(c<=.0031308,c*12.92,1.055*np.maximum(c,0)**(1/2.4)-.055)


def render(base,norm,orm,light):
    L=np.array(light,np.float32);L/=np.linalg.norm(L)
    diff=np.maximum(0,np.sum(norm*L,axis=2))
    ao=orm[...,0]/255
    rough=orm[...,1]/255
    # Broad dielectric response, intended as a local relief diagnostic.
    V=np.array([0,0,1],np.float32)
    H=L+V;H/=np.linalg.norm(H)
    ndh=np.maximum(np.sum(norm*H,axis=2),0)
    spec=.018*ndh**(2/np.maximum(rough,.1)**4)*diff
    rgb=srgb_decode(base/255)*(.33*ao[...,None]+.82*diff[...,None]*(.7+.3*ao[...,None]))+spec[...,None]
    return Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(srgb_encode(rgb)*255),0,255)))


def seams(a):
    if a.ndim==2:a=a[...,None]
    a=a.astype(np.float64)
    out={}
    for axis,label in [(1,'left_right'),(0,'top_bottom')]:
        delta=np.abs(np.diff(a,axis=axis))
        border=np.abs(np.take(a,0,axis=axis)-np.take(a,-1,axis=axis))
        # Distribution of entire interior line mean differences is a fair
        # comparison with the mean difference along the wrap line.
        reduce=(0,2) if axis==1 else (1,2)
        lines=delta.mean(axis=reduce)
        out[label]={'wrap_mean':float(border.mean()),'interior_mean':float(lines.mean()),'interior_line_p95':float(np.percentile(lines,95)),'interior_line_p99':float(np.percentile(lines,99)),'ratio_to_interior_mean':float(border.mean()/max(lines.mean(),1e-9)),'wrap_pixel_p95':float(np.percentile(border,95))}
    return out


def main():
    meta=json.loads((ROOT/'source/build_manifest.json').read_text())
    headers={f:png_header(ROOT/f) for f in FILES}
    ims={f:Image.open(ROOT/f) for f in FILES}
    arrays={f:np.array(im) for f,im in ims.items()}
    base=arrays['thatch_base.png'].astype(np.float32)
    raw_h=arrays['thatch_height.png']
    h=raw_h.astype(np.float64)/65535
    normal=arrays['thatch_normal.png'].astype(np.float64)/255*2-1
    lengths=np.linalg.norm(normal,axis=2)
    normal/=lengths[...,None]
    orm=arrays['thatch_orm.png']
    spacing=1500/N
    dx=(np.roll(h,-1,1)-np.roll(h,1,1))*32/(2*spacing)
    dy=(np.roll(h,-1,0)-np.roll(h,1,0))*32/(2*spacing)
    ref=np.stack((-dx,dy,np.ones_like(h)),axis=2)
    ref/=np.linalg.norm(ref,axis=2,keepdims=True)
    angle=np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip(np.sum(ref*normal,axis=2),-1,1)))
    tie=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/tie_mask.png'))>128
    master=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/registered_master.png'))
    fringe=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/fringe_mask.png'))>128
    ids=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/bundle_ids.png'))
    seam={f:seams(a.astype(np.float32)/(65535 if f=='thatch_height.png' else 255)) for f,a in arrays.items()}
    row_means=(h*32).mean(1)
    body_slopes=[]
    for k in range(4):
        y=np.arange(120,470)
        body_slopes.append(float(np.polyfit(y,row_means[k*512+y],1)[0]*512))
    mean=base.mean((0,1),dtype=np.float64)
    tests={
        'dimensions':all(v['width']==N and v['height']==N for v in headers.values()),
        'height_16bit_greyscale':headers['thatch_height.png']['bit_depth']==16 and headers['thatch_height.png']['colour_type']==0,
        'rgb_8bit':all(headers[f]['bit_depth']==8 and headers[f]['colour_type']==2 for f in [FILES[0],FILES[2],FILES[3]]),
        'srgb_on_base_only':headers[FILES[0]]['icc_profile'] and not any(headers[f]['icc_profile'] for f in FILES[1:]),
        'metalness_zero':bool(np.max(orm[...,2])==0),
        'colour_mean_within_one_code':bool(np.max(np.abs(mean-[190,166,112]))<1),
        'normal_quantization_error_below_half_degree':bool(angle.max()<.5),
        'bundle_id_coverage':bool((ids==0).sum()==0),
        'course_origins':meta['geometry_course_top_rows']==[0,512,1024,1536],
        '11_bundles_per_course':all(len(c['bundles'])==11 for c in meta['courses']),
        'bundle_widths_9_to_15_cm':all(9<=b['width_cm']<=15.02 for c in meta['courses'] for b in c['bundles']),
        'body_no_course_ramp':all(abs(v)<.2 for v in body_slopes),
        'seams_within_interior_p95':all(v['wrap_mean']<=v['interior_line_p95'] for m in seam.values() for v in m.values()),
        'ties_partly_hidden':.35<meta['tie_visible_fraction']<.7,
        'bindings_within_upper_15_percent':all(35<=b['tie_y_px']<=65 for c in meta['courses'] for b in c['bundles']),
    }
    metrics={'units':'Seam statistics are normalized 0..1 channel differences. Height in millimetres. Normal errors in degrees.', 'files':headers,'physical':{'metres_per_tile':1.5,'millimetres_per_pixel':spacing,'height_full_range_mm':32,'height_used_mm':[float(h.min()*32),float(h.max()*32)]},'base_colour_mean_srgb_0_255':mean.tolist(),'base_colour_channel_std':base.std((0,1),dtype=np.float64).tolist(),'base_tied_region_mean':base[np.indices((N,N))[0]%512<75].mean(0).tolist(),'base_body_mean':base[(np.indices((N,N))[0]%512>150)&(np.indices((N,N))[0]%512<425)].mean(0).tolist(),'seams':seam,'normal_height_agreement_degrees':{'mean':float(angle.mean()),'p99':float(np.percentile(angle,99)),'max':float(angle.max())},'decoded_normal_length':{'mean':float(lengths.mean()),'min':float(lengths.min()),'max':float(lengths.max())},'metalness_nonzero_pixels':int(np.count_nonzero(orm[...,2])),'roughness':{'range':(np.array([orm[...,1].min(),orm[...,1].max()])/255).tolist(),'mean':float(orm[...,1].mean()/255),'ties_mean':float(orm[...,1][tie].mean()/255),'straw_mean':float(orm[...,1][~tie].mean()/255)},'visible_tie_pixels':int(tie.sum()),'visible_fraction_of_authored_tie_masks':meta['tie_visible_fraction'],'fringe_pixels':int(fringe.sum()),'unique_bundle_ids':len(np.unique(ids)),'unassigned_bundle_id_pixels':int((ids==0).sum()),'body_height_trend_mm_per_course':body_slopes,'mean_height_jump_at_course_origins_mm':[float(row_means[i]-row_means[(i-1)%N]) for i in [0,512,1024,1536]],'tests':tests}
    (OUT/'metrics.json').write_text(json.dumps(metrics,indent=2)+'\n')
    display={f:(Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(h*255))).convert('RGB') if f=='thatch_height.png' else im.convert('RGB')) for f,im in ims.items()}
    sheet([(f.replace('thatch_',''),display[f]) for f in FILES],'layers.jpg')
    layout=display[FILES[0]].copy()
    ld=ImageDraw.Draw(layout)
    for y in [0,512,1024,1536]:
        ld.line((0,y,N-1,y),fill=(25,105,200),width=2)

    layout.save(OUT/'course_layout.png')
    offsets=[]

        im.save(OUT/f.replace('.png','_offset.png'),**({'icc_profile':ims[f].info['icc_profile']} if f==FILES[0] else {}))
        viz=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(np.array(im)/257))).convert('RGB') if f==FILES[1] else im.convert('RGB')
        offsets.append((f.replace('thatch_','')+' | half offset',viz))

    tiled=np.tile(arrays[FILES[0]],(3,3,1))

    Image.fromarray(tiled).resize((2048,2048),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS).save(OUT/'base_3x3_overview.jpg',quality=95,subsampling=0)
    lights=[('upper_left',[-.65,.65,.72]),('lower_right',[.65,-.55,.72]),('raking_right',[.91,.12,.40])]
    relit=[]
    for name,light in lights:

        im.save(OUT/f'relit_{name}.jpg',quality=97,subsampling=0)
        relit.append((name.replace('_',' ')+' | normal + AO',im))
    sheet(relit,'relighting.jpg',3,768)
    # Neutral clay reveals relief without any base-colour cue.
    clay=render(np.full_like(base,183),normal.astype(np.float32),orm,[-.65,.65,.72])
    clay.save(OUT/'relit_clay.jpg',quality=97,subsampling=0)
    # The exact crop is not resized; 1 output pixel == 1 texture pixel.
    box=(600,430,1368,1198)
    sheet([('base colour | native 1:1',display[FILES[0]].crop(box)),('OpenGL normal | native 1:1',display[FILES[2]].crop(box))],'detail_1to1.png',2,768)
    sheet([('height | native 1:1',display[FILES[1]].crop(box)),('ORM | native 1:1',display[FILES[3]].crop(box))],'detail_data_1to1.png',2,768)
    sheet([('lit | native 1:1',relit[0][1].crop(box)),('clay | native 1:1',clay.crop(box))],'detail_relit_1to1.png',2,768)
    # Shared ID edge overlay helps detect feature registration errors.
    lum=master.astype(np.float32).mean(2)
    edge=(np.abs(np.roll(lum,1,1)-lum)>26)&(np.abs(np.roll(lum,1,0)-lum)<10)
    overlay=np.uint8(base.copy()); overlay[edge]=np.uint8(.58*overlay[edge]+.42*np.array([31,125,204]))
    sheet([('source stem edges / albedo',Image.fromarray(overlay).crop(box)),('height with source edges',Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.asarray(display[FILES[1]])*.7+np.asarray(Image.fromarray(overlay))*.3)).crop(box))],'alignment.png',2,768)
    print(json.dumps({'tests':tests,'mean':mean.tolist(),'normal_degrees':metrics['normal_height_agreement_degrees'],'body_trend_mm':body_slopes,'seam_ratios':{f:{d:round(s['ratio_to_interior_mean'],3) for d,s in v.items()} for f,v in seam.items()}},indent=2))
    if not all(tests.values()): raise SystemExit('Validation failed; see checks/metrics.json')

if __name__=='__main__':

        (OUT/'rebuild.json').write_text(json.dumps(comparison,indent=2)+'\n')
        if not comparison['identical_png_files']:raise SystemExit('Rebuild is not deterministic')
    main()

    lum=np.sum(tex*np.array([.2126,.7152,.0722],np.float32),axis=2)
    broad=gaussian_filter(lum,(17,12),mode='wrap')
    crest=maximum_filter1d(gaussian_filter(lum,.42,mode='wrap'),7,axis=1,mode='wrap')
    # Plateau-style shadow lift, rather than a bilateral display sharpen.
    lifted=lum+np.maximum(0,crest-lum-5)*.96
    liftmean=gaussian_filter(lifted,(18,11),mode='wrap')
    pigment=(lifted-liftmean)*.9
    # Natural straw chroma is low-frequency intrinsic colour; normalize the
    # overall palette and suppress dark-gap desaturation changes.
    chroma=tex-lum[...,None]
    chroma-=gaussian_filter(chroma,(20,12,0),mode='wrap')
    chroma=np.clip(chroma,-10,10)*.32
    col=TARGET+pigment[...,None]*np.array([.85,.94,1.03])+chroma
    # Longitudinal source colour lives in real straw, not directional light.
    low=gaussian_filter(lum,(22,1.1),mode='wrap')-broad
    col+=np.clip(low,-16,16)[...,None]*.22
    return col.astype(np.float32),lum


def native_fibres():
    rng=np.random.default_rng(SEED+101)
    # Native 2048-resolution, elongated epidermal fibres. All filtering wraps.
    noise=rng.normal(0,1,(N,N)).astype(np.float32)
    f=gaussian_filter(noise,(8.2,.42),mode='wrap')
    f-=gaussian_filter(f,(.4,1.7),mode='wrap')
    f/=f.std()
    f=np.clip(f,-2.6,2.6)
    # Coordinate warp follows the slight naturally varying bundle flow.
    yy,xx=np.mgrid[:N,:N].astype(np.float32)
    warp=1.2*np.sin(yy*2*np.pi/N*9+.35*np.sin(xx*2*np.pi/N*13))
    f=map_coordinates(f,[yy,(xx+warp)%N],order=1,mode='grid-wrap')
    # Compose 44 independent full-resolution epidermis patches from the
    # close reed stock. Every patch is sampled at <= its original scale.
    stock=np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_reed_stock.png'),np.float32).mean(2)
    stock-=gaussian_filter(stock,(4.0,1.1),mode='reflect')
    stock=np.clip(stock,-18,18)/max(stock.std(),1)
    patches=[]
    for k in range(4):
        canvas=np.zeros((640,N),np.float32);weights=np.zeros_like(canvas)
        cc=np.asarray(CENTRES[k])*N/PERIOD
        for b,cx in enumerate(cc):
            w=((cc[(b+1)%11]-cc[(b-1)%11])%N)/2
            xp=np.arange(int(cx-w*.78),int(cx+w*.78)+1)
            dx=xp-cx
            wt=np.maximum(0,np.cos(np.clip(dx/(w*.78),-1,1)*np.pi/2))**2
            sy=rng.uniform(5,350)+np.arange(640,dtype=np.float32)[:,None]*1.35
            sx=rng.uniform(250,1000)+dx[None,:]*1.3+1.3*np.sin(sy/100+rng.uniform(0,6))
            patch=map_coordinates(stock,[np.broadcast_to(sy,sx.shape),sx],order=1,mode='reflect')
            canvas[:,xp%N]+=patch*wt[None,:]
            weights[:,xp%N]+=wt[None,:]
        patches.append(canvas/np.maximum(weights,.001))
    skin=np.empty((N,N),np.float32)
    blend=smooth(0,115,np.arange(128,dtype=np.float32))[:,None]
    for k in range(4):
        row=patches[k][:512].copy()
        row[:128]=patches[(k-1)%4][512:640]*(1-blend)+row[:128]*blend
        skin[k*512:(k+1)*512]=row
    skin/=skin.std()
    f=.72*skin+.5*f
    f/=f.std()
    return np.clip(f,-3,3)


def build():
    t=time.monotonic()
    tex,registration=register_source()
    tex=register_bindings(tex)
    tex,fringe,proud,fringe_meta=overlap_fringes(tex)
    save_rgb('source/registered_master.png',tex,True)
    crown,tie,bundle,courses=geometry_fields(tex)
    tie_uncovered=tie.copy()
    tie*=1-fringe
    base,lum=intrinsic_colour(tex)
    f=native_fibres()
    # No generation/upscale-only claim: this 2K fibre field is new material
    # detail and contributes coherently to pigment and physical displacement.
    base+=f[...,None]*3.8
    cy=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float32)[:,None]%512
    weather=np.maximum(smooth(435,505,cy),fringe)
    base+=weather[...,None]*np.array([-1.,.7,5.5])
    base+=TARGET-base.mean((0,1),dtype=np.float64)
    save_rgb('thatch_base.png',base,True)
    # Locally normalized ridge signal, no low-frequency course lighting.
    local=lum-gaussian_filter(lum,(6.0,4.0),mode='wrap')
    contrast=np.sqrt(gaussian_filter(local**2,(12,12),mode='wrap')+36)
    ridges=np.tanh(local/contrast*.68)
    h=8.5+crown+1.35*ridges+tie*.85+f*.07+proud
    h=gaussian_filter(h,.42,mode='wrap')
    h16=np.uint16(np.rint(np.clip(h/HEIGHT_MM,0,1)*65535))
    Image.fromarray(h16).save(ROOT/'thatch_height.png')
    hq=h16.astype(np.float32)/65535
    save_rgb('thatch_normal.png',(normals(hq)*.5+.5)*255)
    # Gap and overhang occlusion is isolated in red. The source's broad
    # brightness is never copied wholesale to AO or height.
    valleys=np.clip(-ridges,0,1)
    micro=np.exp(-.32*valleys)
    y=np.arange(N,dtype=np.float32)[:,None]%512
    tied_shadow=1-.47*(1-fringe)*smooth(0,22,y)*(1-smooth(55,128,y))
    cavity=np.maximum(0,gaussian_filter(h,(5,9),mode='wrap')-h)
    ao=np.clip(micro*tied_shadow*np.exp(-cavity*.055),.26,1)
    rough=.86+.045*valleys+.013*f+.025*weather
    rough=np.clip(rough,.78,.95)
    rough=rough*(1-tie)+(.765+.012*f)*tie
    rough=np.clip(rough,.75,.95)
    save_rgb('thatch_orm.png',np.stack([ao,rough,np.zeros_like(ao)],-1)*255)
    Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(tie*255))).save(ROOT/'source/tie_mask.png')
    Image.fromarray(bundle).save(ROOT/'source/bundle_ids.png')
    Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(fringe*255))).save(ROOT/'source/fringe_mask.png')
    Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.rint(tie_uncovered*255))).save(ROOT/'source/tie_mask_before_overlap.png')
    Image.fromarray(np.uint16(np.rint((np.clip(f,-3,3)+3)/6*65535))).save(ROOT/'source/native_fibre_field.png')
    save_rgb('source/pigment_master.png',base,True)

+def mip_average(a,size=200):
+    """Area average each data channel, preserving floating-point samples."""
+    if a.ndim==2:
+        return np.asarray(Image.fromarray(a.astype(np.float32)).resize((size,size),Image.Resampling.BOX))
+    return np.stack([mip_average(a[...,c],size) for c in range(a.shape[2])],-1)
+
+
+def comparison_checks(base,normal,orm,h,meta):
+    """Compare retained maps at native resolution and an actual 200px mip.
+
+    The distance preview filters linear colour and linear data BEFORE shading,
+    then renormalizes normals. This tests whether broad bundle features survive
+    texture minification, without pretending to reproduce the game's mesh.
+    """
+    oldmeta=json.loads((ROOT/'source/round1_build_manifest.json').read_text())
+    oldmetrics=json.loads((ROOT/'source/round1_metrics.json').read_text())
+    old={f:np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'round1'/f)) for f in FILES}
+    oldnormal=old[FILES[2]].astype(np.float32)/255*2-1
+    oldnormal/=np.linalg.norm(oldnormal,axis=2,keepdims=True)
+    rounds=[(old[FILES[0]].astype(np.float32),oldnormal,old[FILES[3]],old[FILES[1]].astype(np.float64)/65535),
+            (base,normal.astype(np.float32),orm,h)]
+    native=[];small=[];walls=[];stats=[]
+    for i,(col,n,packed,height) in enumerate(rounds,1):
+        linear=srgb_decode(col/255)
+        b=srgb_encode(mip_average(linear))*255
+        nm=mip_average(n);nm/=np.linalg.norm(nm,axis=2,keepdims=True)
+        om=mip_average(packed)
+        albedo=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(b),0,255)))
+        small.append((f'Round {i} | base',albedo))
+        native.append((f'Round {i} | base colour, full 2048px tile at 1:1',Image.fromarray(np.uint8(col))))
+        walls.append((f'Round {i} | 3 m wall, base',Image.fromarray(np.tile(np.asarray(albedo),(2,2,1)))))
+        lum=b@np.array([.2126,.7152,.0722])
+        values=[];course_means=[]
+        for k,course in enumerate(meta['courses']):
+            y0=int((k*512+170)*200/N);y1=int((k*512+425)*200/N)
+            course_means.append(float(lum[y0:y1].mean()))
+            bundles=[]
+            for bundle in course['bundles']:
+                cx=bundle['centre_px']*200/N;w=bundle['width_px']*200/N

+    # Keep paired columns for each treatment.

+    sheet(small,'round_comparison_200px.png',2,200,32)

+def mip_average(a,size=200):
+    """Area average each data channel, preserving floating-point samples."""
+    if a.ndim==2:
+        return np.asarray(Image.fromarray(a.astype(np.float32)).resize((size,size),Image.Resampling.BOX))
+    return np.stack([mip_average(a[...,c],size) for c in range(a.shape[2])],-1)
+
+
+def comparison_checks(base,normal,orm,h,meta):
+    """Compare retained maps at native resolution and an actual 200px mip.
+
+    The distance preview filters linear colour and linear data BEFORE shading,
+    then renormalizes normals. This tests whether broad bundle features survive
+    texture minification, without pretending to reproduce the game's mesh.
+    """
+    oldmeta=json.loads((ROOT/'source/round1_build_manifest.json').read_text())
+    oldmetrics=json.loads((ROOT/'source/round1_metrics.json').read_text())
+    old={f:np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'round1'/f)) for f in FILES}
+    oldnormal=old[FILES[2]].astype(np.float32)/255*2-1
+    oldnormal/=np.linalg.norm(oldnormal,axis=2,keepdims=True)
+    rounds=[(old[FILES[0]].astype(np.float32),oldnormal,old[FILES[3]],old[FILES[1]].astype(np.float64)/65535),
+            (base,normal.astype(np.float32),orm,h)]
+    native=[];small=[];walls=[];stats=[]
+    for i,(col,n,packed,height) in enumerate(rounds,1):
+        linear=srgb_decode(col/255)
+        b=srgb_encode(mip_average(linear))*255
+        nm=mip_average(n);nm/=np.linalg.norm(nm,axis=2,keepdims=True)
+        om=mip_average(packed)
+        albedo=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(b),0,255)))
+        small.append((f'Round {i} | base',albedo))
+        native.append((f'Round {i} | base colour, full 2048px tile at 1:1',Image.fromarray(np.uint8(col))))
+        walls.append((f'Round {i} | 3 m wall, base',Image.fromarray(np.tile(np.asarray(albedo),(2,2,1)))))
+        lum=b@np.array([.2126,.7152,.0722])
+        values=[];course_means=[]
+        for k,course in enumerate(meta['courses']):
+            y0=int((k*512+170)*200/N);y1=int((k*512+425)*200/N)
+            course_means.append(float(lum[y0:y1].mean()))
+            bundles=[]
+            for bundle in course['bundles']:
+                cx=bundle['centre_px']*200/N;w=bundle['width_px']*200/N
+                xs=np.arange(int(np.floor(cx-w*.23)),int(np.ceil(cx+w*.23)))%200
+                bundles.append(float(lum[y0:y1,xs].mean()))
